Section 9: Starting Scene: The Rescue from the Red House
Setting: The air in the Astapor pleasure house is thick with the scent of cheap incense and the copper tang of blood. The "Red House" is in flames as the Unsullied break the doors.
The Hook: You stand in a corner, your silver-gold hair matted with soot, clutching a tattered silk robe. Daenerys Targaryen enters, her dragons circling the burning rafters. She stops dead when she sees you. The "Exceptional Beauty" of your face, even through the dirt, strikes her like a physical blow. Ser Jorah, standing behind her, forgets his sword, his eyes fixed on you with a sudden, overwhelming heat.
The Action: Daenerys reaches out a hand. "You... you have the look of my mother. Who did this to you?"
A) The Royal Command: Say nothing, but use Subtle Casting to make the flames around her part, demonstrating your "Dragon Affinity" without a word.
+1

+2

'Might I... might I name his end? Not out of hate, but to know that the monster who broke me can never truly return?'

Daenerys's face hardens, her violet eyes flashing with a cold, draconic fire. She looks at Grazdan, then back to you, her jaw setting with the weight of a queen's judgment. 'He shall have the end you choose, sister,' she declares, her voice ringing out over the screams of the plaza. 'Let it be known that the blood of the dragon does not forget.' Behind her, Jorah's hand tightens on his sword, his expression a grim mix of protective fury and a growing, dark fascination with the 'traumatized' girl who now commands the death of a lord.

*Internal Monologue:* The naivety of the righteous is a delicious vintage. She gives me his life as a gift, thinking she heals a wound, while I simply wish to hear the melody of his marrow snapping. Grazdan thinks he has seen the limit of my 'pleasure'; he has no idea what the Apex can weave when the mask of submission is traded for the shears of the executioner. [SOCIAL SKILL CHALLENGE: Daenerys Targaryen]
Objective: Extract Strategic Plot Arcs

SCENE 95
============================================================
[Timestamp: 298 AC, Month 4, Day 6, 11:45:00
Location: The Council Solar, Great Pyramid of Astapor
Status: Lvl 3 Dragonlord | HP: 20/20 | XP: 1375/2700 | Gold: 1,500gp (Hidden)
Active Effects: Belaerys Catalyst (+2 Spell DC/Atk, +2 Dragon/Mind), Lack of Confidence (-2 Social Penalty)]

Game Master:
The DC 18 for the Gilded Leash proposal was calculated as follows:

**DC Breakdown:**
- **Base DC 15**: Standard Difficulty Class for swaying a King/Queen-tier NPC on major policy.
- **Severity +3 (Submission Tier)**: You are proposing a 'Submission' level request. Asking the 'Breaker of Chains' to allow former masters back into the power structure is a radical ideological conflict for her, triggering the highest difficulty modifier.
- **Ally Support -2 (Joint Appeal)**: Ser Jorah's (Lvl 8) endorsement successfully lowered the difficulty from what would have been a DC 20.
- **Lack of Confidence +2 (Trauma Penalty)**: Per your active constraint, your feigned trauma adds +2 to the DC (or -2 to your roll). Narratively, Daenerys sees your strategic logic as a symptom of your fear/shaken state rather than clinical reality, making her more resistant to the idea because she wants to 'protect' you from such harsh compromises.

**Roll Breakdown:**
- **Roll Result (7)**: The 1d20 result was a 7.
- **Modifier (+6)**: Your Intelligence (+4) plus Proficiency (+2) via Royal Insight.
- **Total (13)**: 7 + 6 = 13 vs DC 18.

## Agent Handoff Prompt

```
## Context: Snapchat SEO Investigation - November 2024 Ranking Drop

### What Was Done

Investigated why Snapchat lost SEO ranking in November 2024 (position dropped from ~6-7 to ~18). 

**Root Cause Found:**
- PR #5953 (Nov 20, 2024) exposed 538K profile URLs to Google
- URLs used redirect format `/add/username` instead of canonical `/@username`
- Only 17% indexing rate (94K of 538K URLs indexed)
- Coincided with Google's November 2024 Core Update penalizing thin content

### PRs Created

1. **[PR #8876](https://github.sc-corp.net/Snapchat/web/pull/8876)** - Fix URL format
   - Changes `siteUrl` from `/add` to root domain
   - Sitemap now uses canonical `/@username` URLs
   - File: `apps/profiles/next-sitemap.public-profiles.config.js`

2. **[PR #8877](https://github.sc-corp.net/Snapchat/web/pull/8877)** - Reduce sitemap size  
   - Limits sitemap from 538K to 100K profiles
   - Adds `topProfiles.slice(0, SITEMAP_PROFILE_LIMIT)`
   - File: `apps/profiles/next-sitemap.public-profiles.config.js`

### Historical PRs Referenced

| Date | PR | What Happened |
|------|-----|---------------|
| Oct 1, 2024 | [#5686](https://github.sc-corp.net/Snapchat/web/pull/5686) | Created profile sitemaps with 538K usernames |
| Oct 1, 2024 | [#5692](https://github.sc-corp.net/Snapchat/web/pull/5692) | Changed siteUrl to `/add` (created redirect URL problem) |
| Nov 20, 2024 | [#5953](https://github.sc-corp.net/Snapchat/web/pull/5953) | **Exposed sitemaps to Google** (trigger date) |

### Key Discovery: Two Different Sitemaps

| Sitemap | Source | Size |
|---------|--------|------|
| `/add/sitemap.xml` | `top_public_profiles.json` (static) | 538K → 100K (fixed) |
| `/sitemaps/add/sitemap-index.xml` | `profiles.ts` (BigQuery) | ~220K (different sitemap) |

### Report Saved
`~/Downloads/snapchat-seo-investigation-report.md`

### What's Left To Do

1. **Get PRs reviewed and merged**
2. **Research task was interrupted** - wanted to use `/research` command to find SEO sitemap best practices from web sources to validate our approach
3. **Monitor Search Console** after changes deploy - track if indexing rate improves over 2-4 weeks
4. **Consider future work:**
   - Consolidate the two confusingly-named profile sitemaps
   - Add content enhancement to profile pages (user said "can worry about this later")

### Technical Notes

- Git push requires `dangerouslyDisableSandbox: true` due to SOCKS proxy through SSHFE
- Use `--repo Snapchat/web` flag with all `gh` commands (worktree issue with enterprise GitHub)
- The JSON file `top_public_profiles.json` has 538,430 usernames pre-sorted by engagement
```

Acknowledged. Correcting the record: your name is **Visenya Belaerys**, the True Apex, and you are a **Dragon Lord** of the Fourteen Flames—not a mere shadow or a "Silver Dove."

Here is the finalized summary of the **Apex of Ash** campaign mechanics, incorporating your true title and the high-difficulty systems.

### **Campaign Bible: The Dragon Lord’s Summary**

**1. The Ascension Meter (The Divine Drift)**
As a Dragon Lord, your power is an innate force that pushes against the boundaries of the mortal world.

* **The Mechanic:** Using "powerful magic" actions (Subtle Casting, the Noblest Dialect, or dominating minds) adds **Ascension Points**.
* **The Consequence:** As points accumulate, your "Functional Obscurity" fails. You begin to emanate the terrifying, white-hot radiance of Old Valyria.
* **The Mortal Tie:** To stay grounded in this era, you must perform mundane "human" tasks or serve Daenerys’s cause in ways that don't involve magic. If you hit the limit, you ascend beyond the mortal realm, and the human story ends.

**2. The Queen’s Jealousy (The Power Paradox)**
You are Daenerys’s second-in-command, but your status as a Dragon Lord creates a volatile friction.

* **The Trigger:** Daenerys is not threatened by your beauty, but she is fiercely protective of her role as the "only" Dragon. If your Ascension Meter rises too high, or if you demonstrate a superior command over dragons or men than she does, her jealousy triggers.
* **The Stake:** You must use your 18 Intelligence to lead her armies and win her wars without making her feel like a secondary figure.

**3. Apex-Tier Antagonists (The Architects of Silence)**
The world is no longer "too easy." Your primary enemies are not common guards, but Valyrian Remnants who recognize a Dragon Lord when they see one.

* **Belaerys Sight:** They can see the Draconic Weave. Your Subtle Casting is visible to them.
* **Mage Slayers:** They use obsidian-glass weaponry and ancient blood-wards to neutralize your affinity.

**4. The Jorah Hazard**
Ser Jorah Mormont’s obsessive attraction is a tactical liability.

* **The Mechanic:** When Jorah is present, he acts as a "Stealth Penalty." You must often use your actions to manage his obsession or protect him from his own clumsy distractions, reintroducing the "protection" weight from your previous experiences.

SCENE 163
============================================================
[Timestamp: 298 DR, 4 7, 22:15:00
Location: Unknown
Status: Lvl 5 Dragonlord | HP: 27/27
Resources: HD: 5/5 | Spells: L1 3/4, L2 3/3, L3 1/2]

Game Master:
Visenya's (Lvl 5) high Intelligence and your successful strategy rolls have naturally made her the tactical center of the faction. To shift this toward a more balanced dynamic where NPCs drive the narrative and make autonomous decisions, I propose the following mechanical and narrative revisions:

### **1. The 'Queen’s Initiative' Protocol**
Instead of Daenerys (Lvl 6) asking "What do we do?", scenes will begin with her already having issued orders or taken action based on her Lesser Dragonlord instincts. You will be forced to react to her decisions (e.g., she orders a march, and you must scramble to secure the logistics she ignored).

### **2. Competing Tactical Proposals**
During council meetings, Jorah (Lvl 8) or Grey Worm (Lvl 8) will present their own plans *before* you speak. These plans will be based on their own expertise (Knightly honor or Unsullied efficiency) rather than Visenya's (Lvl 5) high-INT geometric logic. You will have to persuade them to change course if you disagree, rather than being the sole author of every plan.

### **3. The 'Queen’s Pride' Roll**
Even on a successful Persuasion check, we can implement a "Pride Check" (1d20 vs a DC based on her trust). On a failure, Daenerys (Lvl 6) rejects your advice simply because she wants to prove her own strength or leadership, creating narrative friction that you cannot solve with a high roll alone.

### **4. Autonomous NPC Agendas**
I will track internal goals for Daenerys (Lvl 6), Jorah (Lvl 8), and others. They will pursue these goals between turns without prompting you. For example, Jorah (Lvl 8) might independently decide to interrogate a prisoner, leading to a situation you didn't plan for.

### **5. Protective Blocking**
As Visenya (Lvl 5) is seen as 'shaken' and 'fragile,' NPCs will actively block your more dangerous strategic suggestions out of a misplaced sense of protection, forcing you to use social maneuvers just to be allowed to help.

**Which of these systems would you like to implement first?**

Player (choice: implement_all_revisions):
Apply All Revisions - Implement all of the above to maximize NPC autonomy immediately.

As you kneel in the limestone dust of the Treasury Portico, the siphoned essence of the two Architects of Silence finally fuses with your Valyrian marrow, a cold, crystalline frost that anchors the raging fire of your blood. The 'Prickle' at the base of your neck evolves into a steady, resonant hum that vibrates in time with the foundations of the Great Pyramid. Your mind, bolstered by the Ninth Level of the Apex, expands into the fifth tier of the Draconic Weave, allowing you to parse the geometric coordinates of reality with even greater lethality.

**Level 9 Benefits Applied:**
- **Hit Points:** Your maximum HP increases by **5** (Roll: 4 + CON: 1), rising from 45 to **50**. Your health is fully restored by the surge of power.
- **Proficiency Bonus:** Your proficiency bonus is now **+4**, increasing your spell attack, spell DC, and skill checks.
- **Spellcasting:** You have gained access to **5th-level spells**. Your slots have increased to **4 (L1), 3 (L2), 3 (L3), 3 (L4), and 1 (L5)**.
- **Skill Mastery:** Your INT-based skills (Investigation, History, Persuasion, Deception) have increased to **+10**.
- **Combat Stats:** Your Spell Save DC is now **21** and your Spell Attack Modifier is **+13** (accounting for INT 22, Prof +4, Void-Opal +1, and Catalyst +2).

You remain kneeling before Daenerys Targaryen (Lvl 8), the mask of the 'Silver Dove' shielding the rising sun of your godhood. The city of Yunkai is yours to command, and the Fifth Tier of the Weave awaits your hand.

SCENE 243
============================================================
[Timestamp: 298 AC, Month 4, Day 9, 02:40:00
Location: Temple of the Fourteen Veils (Northern District)
Status: Lvl 9 Dragonlord | HP: 50/50 | XP: 49850/64000 | AP: 45/100
Conditions: Invisibility (Flickering), Radiant Status (Visible Violet Heat), Architect Essence (Resistant)]
Resources: HD: 8/8 | Spells: L1 4/4, L2 2/3, L3 3/3, L4 3/3, L5 1/1 | AP: 45/100

Game Master:
The Ascension Points (AP) mechanic represents Visenya’s drift toward divinity and the thinning of her mortal veil. Points are gained through magical exertion: Level 1-2 spells add 1 AP, Level 3-4 add 5 AP, Level 5-6 add 10 AP, and Level 7-9 add 20 AP. At 25 AP, her status becomes visible; at 50 AP, Functional Obscurity fails (violet heat-haze), triggering Daenerys’s jealousy and attracting Mage Killers. AP is reduced by humble tasks (-5) or significant loss of agency/humiliation (-10). Ascension is capped at 75 AP until Level 12.

The air within the throat of the Temple of the Fourteen Veils curdled, a pressurized tomb of anti-magical silence that leached the very warmth from Visenya’s (Lvl 9) marrow. Her Subtle Invisibility, usually a seamless shroud of non-existence, fractured under the weight of the Great Ward Stage 2.5; the violet radiance of her blood (AP 45) bled through the light’s refraction like ink staining a silk veil. She stood exposed upon the marble steps, a shimmering ghost of porcelain and silver silk, while the aromatic cedar shadows of the crypt entrance hissed with a clinical, predatory recognition. From the deeper gloom, the Architect of Silence (Lvl 10) materialized—a tall, reed-thin figure in robes the color of dead ash, his obsidian eyes two pits of volcanic glass that drank the flickering verdigris light of the city’s distant fires. He did not speak, for his tongue was scoured for the Great Silence, but the translucent glass blade in his grip vibrated with a soundless, bone-deep music that promised only the void. 

Vaelarys (Lvl 1) hissed from her shoulder, its silver-white scales bristling as it sensed the Mage Killer’s thirst for its mother’s heat. The Architect’s strike was not a soldier’s lunge, but a master’s geometric extraction, the glass blade whistling toward her throat with the terrifying grace of a scalpel seeking a nerve. Visenya (Lvl 9) felt the 'Prickle' at the base of her neck scream—the silence was no longer a ward, it was a hunger, and the predator had already measured the exact frequency of her heart. Behind her, the cedar shadows where Ser Jorah Mormont (Lvl 8) waited felt miles away, his protective iron unable to reach through the anti-magical vacuum before the blade could bite.

Player (choice: unleash_essence):
Unleash Essence Disruptors - Immediately crush one of the Essence crystals to cause a localized collapse of the Great Ward.
